<script>
  (function() {
    var rocketLoaded = false;
    var blockedScripts = [];
    var origCreateElement = document.createElement.bind(document);

    document.createElement = function(tag) {
      var el = origCreateElement(tag);
      if (typeof tag === 'string' && tag.toLowerCase() === 'script') {
        var origSetAttr = el.setAttribute.bind(el);
        Object.defineProperty(el, 'src', {
          configurable: true,
          set: function(val) {
            var srcStr = typeof val === 'string'
              ? val
              : (val && typeof val.toString === 'function' ? val.toString() : '');

            if (srcStr.indexOf('pushdaddy') !== -1) {
              return;
            }
            if (!rocketLoaded && srcStr.indexOf('entanglecommerce.com') !== -1) {
              blockedScripts.push(srcStr);
              return;
            }
            origSetAttr('src', val);
          },
          get: function() {
            return el.getAttribute('src');
          }
        });
      }
      return el;
    };

    function loadRocketReviews() {
      if (rocketLoaded) return;

        var s = origCreateElement('script');

        s.async = true;
